  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gajah Mada, leader of Indonesia (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Indonesia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Kris Swordsman, Replacing Swordsman (Requires technology: Iron Working)
        - Unique Unit: Djong, Replacing Galleass (Requires technology: Guilds)
        - Unique Building: Candi, Replacing Garden (Requires technology: Theology)
        - Unique Improvement: Kampong (Unique Indonesian Improvement. The Kampong can be built by Embarked Workers on Coastal and Lake Tiles, and not next to another Kampong.
          Each Kampong yields Food, Production, and Culture.); Requires technology: Sailing
        - Civilization Ability: When you gain a City, one of 3 unique Luxuries (//) will appear nearby. +5% to unique Yield and Golden Age duration modifiers from Global Monopolies; +2 to Yields and Happiness from Global Monopolies.
      - Leader: Gajah Mada
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Spirit (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Martial Spirit: +25% Combat Strength when attacking in melee for 50 turns, scaling with game speed. War Weariness reduced by 25%, and Razing Speed is doubled.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - Third Alternative (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Third Alternative: Quantity of Strategic Resources produced (including Paper) is increased by 100%. Reduces Unit Gold Maintenance costs by 25%.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 4
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 5
        - WarBias: 4
        - HostileBias: 5
        - WarmongerHate: 9

        - WorkWithWillingness: 9
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 7
        - Loyalty: 9
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 2
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 1
        - MinorCivWarBias: 1
        - DenounceWillingness: 4
        - Forgiveness: 5
        - Meanness: 4
        - Neediness: 5
        - Chattiness: 10
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: DIPLOMATIC VICTORY PERSONA MAINTAINED: Critical 1-turn voting window requires maximum delegate protection. VictoryCompetitiveness(10) and MinorCivCompetitiveness(10) essential for tight 3-way race (27 vs 28 vs 28 delegates). High MinorCivFriendlyBias(10) protects Prague, Wittenberg, Winchester alliances providing 6 delegates. Wittenberg Hinduism quest has ONLY 4 turns remaining - must complete before vote. Moderate Boldness(5) and WarBias(4) maintain measured defense against Aztecs/Shoshone while avoiding Iroquois escalation. High Friendliness(9), WorkWithWillingness(9), Chattiness(10) enable diplomatic maneuvering during World Congress voting.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Electronics
        - Rationale: Electronics unlocks Police Stations (+10 City Security, +3 Culture) critical for defending cities against Aztec/Shoshone aggression during World Congress voting. Leads to Telecommunications then Satellites/Apollo Program for late-game diplomatic/science backup options. City security vital when managing multiple active conflicts and protecting delegate sources. Police Stations synergize with Autocracy tenets (New World Order, Police State) for production bonuses. Currently completing Advanced Ballistics (~8 turns), so this sets next research queue position appropriately.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Enlightenment (Policy)
        - Rationale: Enlightenment (Rationalism branch) provides FREE TECHNOLOGY - accelerates victory timeline significantly by immediately advancing toward Satellites/Apollo Program or other critical late-game techs. +1 Happiness from Universities helps maintain stability during wartime with multiple active conflicts. Policy available in ~14 turns, positioning us well for endgame. Rationalism completion supports science output for late-game tech race while pursuing diplomatic victory. Free tech could unlock Satellites faster for Science Victory backup plan if diplomatic route becomes compromised.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Japan
        - Public: -30
        - Private: -40
        - Rationale: Japan has denounced us and we've denounced them. They have Authority branch complete and are at war with Iroquois and Brazil. While not our primary concern during diplomatic victory push, maintaining negative relationship reflects mutual denunciations and divergent interests. Japan has only 2 delegates so they're less critical for World Congress, but their military strength warrants caution. Keeping relationship negative ensures AI maintains appropriate defensive posture.
        - UpdatedTurn: 247
        
        ### Sofia
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Sofia just became our ally (430 influence) and declared war on Aztecs, showing strong alignment with our interests. As a city-state, they provide 2 delegates. Maintaining maximum positive relationship ensures they remain loyal through World Congress voting. Their military action against our enemy demonstrates shared strategic interests.
        - UpdatedTurn: 303
        
        ### The Celts
        - Public: -80
        - Private: -60
        - Rationale: CELTS - WARTIME ENEMY BUT POTENTIAL LIBERATION ALLY: Currently at war with them (100% war score against us), but they are the ONLY potential ally against Iroquois hegemony. Their -80 public reflects active warfare, but -60 private leaves room for post-war reconciliation. Their 627 military strength could be valuable if we can coordinate against Iroquois after World Congress vote. Need to end this war strategically to free up military resources and potentially gain an ally against Iroquois.
        - UpdatedTurn: 411
        
        ### The Iroquois
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: IROQUOIS - EXISTENTIAL THREAT MAXIMIZED: They possess 377k military vs my 6.8k, control 87% of original capitals (7/8), lead World Congress with 28 delegates (1 ahead of my 27), and have atomic bombs positioned near my territories. As my suzerain (capitulated vassal), they pose ultimate threat to sovereignty. Maximum negative relationship ensures AI maintains highest defensive alert, prioritizes nuclear deterrent development, and prepares for potential liberation scenarios post-vote. Critical to secure diplomatic victory before any military confrontation - their air superiority near coastal cities requires maximum defensive posture.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### The Aztecs
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: AZTECS - ACTIVE WAR ENEMY URGENT: They conquered our city of Denpasar, naval forces actively assault our coastal territories. We declared war and captured Tlatelolco. Maximum negative ensures AI prioritizes defending coastal cities (Medan, Makassar, Batam vulnerable) and supports offensive operations to reclaim Denpasar. Their 2,924 military strength is manageable but dangerous with naval units actively attacking. Must secure territory before/during World Congress vote - losing cities during voting could cost us the diplomatic victory in this tight 27 vs 28 vs 28 delegate race.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### The Shoshone
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: SHOSHONE - ACTIVE WAR ENEMY PRIORITY: They declared war on Indonesia with 2,961 military strength, units actively attacking our territory. They denounced Celts (complicating our diplomacy) and follow Freedom ideology (opposing our Autocracy). Maximum negative ensures AI prioritizes defense against Shoshone aggression and supports offensive operations to eliminate this threat before World Congress vote. Their naval bombardment capability threatens coastal cities. Must secure territory to free military resources for Iroquois deterrence and protect city-state allies providing critical delegates.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Prague
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: PRAGUE - CRITICAL CITY-STATE ALLY: At 1031 influence (well above ~200 alliance threshold), they provide 2 delegates for World Congress. Currently at war with Aztecs, Iroquois, Poland, Brazil, and Shoshone - all our enemies or rivals. Their naval units help defend our waters. Technology quest has 1 turn remaining - we lead 10-4, should complete soon. Maximum positive relationship ensures AI prioritizes protecting this alliance. Critical delegate source that must remain loyal through World Congress voting against Brazilian Sphere of Influence proposals. Losing this ally would cost us 2 delegates - potentially decisive in tight 27 vs 28 vs 28 race.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Wittenberg
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: WITTENBERG - URGENT CITY-STATE ALLY: At 656 influence (above alliance threshold), they provide 2 delegates. Hinduism quest has ONLY 4 TURNS REMAINING - this is CRITICAL priority. Must complete before World Congress vote to secure their loyalty. Currently at war with Shoshone and Aztecs (our enemies). Maximum positive relationship ensures AI prioritizes completing their religion quest immediately. Losing this ally to Brazilian Sphere of Influence pressure would cost us 2 delegates - potentially decisive in tight race. Hinduism missionaries may need deployment to Wittenberg urgently.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Winchester
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: WINCHESTER - CRITICAL CITY-STATE ALLY: At 336 influence (above alliance threshold), they provide 2 delegates for World Congress vote. Currently at war with Shoshone and Aztecs (our enemies). Great Artist quest has 43 turns - lower priority but alliance must be preserved. Maximum positive relationship ensures AI prioritizes protecting this alliance. Brazil's sanction proposals and sphere of influence demands threaten city-state independence - must maintain strongest possible bond to prevent losing these critical delegates. Combined with Prague and Wittenberg, these 3 city-states provide 6 delegates - essential for diplomatic victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Poland
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: POLAND - FRIENDLY NEIGHBOR WITH CAUTION: Poland stole our tiles previously but we have Declaration of Friendship and both follow Autocracy. They have only 16 delegates (not a diplomatic threat). Positive relationship reflects potential utility as counterweight to Iroquois/Brazil influence. Their 638 military is weak but territory theft is concerning. Public 40 shows friendship, private 20 reflects underlying caution. May be useful for post-vote diplomatic maneuvering. Both share Autocracy ideology which provides diplomatic bonuses. Currently at war with Shoshone (our enemy) which aligns interests.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -60
        - Rationale: BRAZIL - DIPLOMATIC RIVAL ESCALATED: Brazil has 26 delegates (3 behind my 29), actively pushing Sphere of Influence proposals threatening my city-state alliances. They just LOST Zanzibar to me this turn - they will try to reclaim. Moderate-negative relationship increased to reflect heightened competition during critical voting phase. Brazil has strong economy (8364 gold, +541 GPT) enabling aggressive diplomatic unit purchases. Must ensure AI prioritizes countering Brazilian diplomatic pressure on city-states like Belgrade (444 influence, not yet ally) and protects existing alliances from Brazilian influence campaigns.
        - UpdatedTurn: 424
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: PERSONA MAINTAINED: Current diplomatic victory persona remains optimal. VictoryCompetitiveness(10) and MinorCivCompetitiveness(10) essential for tight 3-way delegate race. MinorCivFriendlyBias(10) protects city-state alliances (Prague 1031, Wittenberg 656, Winchester 336, Zanzibar 674 - all allies providing 8 delegates total). High Friendliness(9), WorkWithWillingness(9), Chattiness(10) enable diplomatic maneuvering during World Congress voting. Moderate Boldness(5) and WarBias(4) maintain measured defense against Aztecs/Shoshone while avoiding Iroquois escalation. Relationships with Iroquois(-100/-100), Aztecs(-100/-100), Shoshone(-100/-100) remain at maximum hostility reflecting active wars and existential threats.

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 41
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 10 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - World Religion: Confucianism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Indonesian Sphere of Influence: Prague:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Global Peace Accords:
            - Description: Unit maintenance costs are raised by 25% Gold. 2.0x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 0.5x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Indonesian Sphere of Influence: Winchester:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Sokoto:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Indonesian Sphere of Influence: Wittenberg:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - EnactedOn: 424
          - World Ideology: Autocracy:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Cape Town:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Raqmu:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Global Hegemony:
            - Description: Designate a player that could achieve Global Hegemony. If a Civilization receives support from enough Delegates in the Congress, that leader will attain Diplomatic Victory. The number of Delegates needed depends on the number of Civilizations and City-States in the game, and can be seen on the World Congress screen.
              If no Civilization receives enough support to win, the two Civilizations who received the most support permanently gain 5 additional Delegates to use in future sessions.
              May not be proposed by a Civilization. Automatically proposed at regular intervals once a World Ideology Resolution is active.
            - Proposer: Unknown

        You, Gajah Mada (leader of Indonesia, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 425.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gajah Mada, leader of Indonesia (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Indonesia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Kris Swordsman, Replacing Swordsman (Requires technology: Iron Working)
        - Unique Unit: Djong, Replacing Galleass (Requires technology: Guilds)
        - Unique Building: Candi, Replacing Garden (Requires technology: Theology)
        - Unique Improvement: Kampong (Unique Indonesian Improvement. The Kampong can be built by Embarked Workers on Coastal and Lake Tiles, and not next to another Kampong.
          Each Kampong yields Food, Production, and Culture.); Requires technology: Sailing
        - Civilization Ability: When you gain a City, one of 3 unique Luxuries (//) will appear nearby. +5% to unique Yield and Golden Age duration modifiers from Global Monopolies; +2 to Yields and Happiness from Global Monopolies.
      - Leader: Gajah Mada
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late world Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Spirit (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Martial Spirit: +25% Combat Strength when attacking in melee for 50 turns, scaling with world speed. War Weariness reduced by 25%, and Razing Speed is doubled.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - Third Alternative (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Third Alternative: Quantity of Strategic Resources produced (including Paper) is increased by 100%. Reduces Unit Gold Maintenance costs by 25%.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Electronics
        - Rationale: Electronics remains optimal next research. Unlocks Police Stations (+10 City Security, +3 Culture) critical for defending core cities against Iroquois infiltration and border fortification visible in event logs. Completing it in ~8 positions us to use the imminent Enlightenment free technology on Satellites for Apollo Program access—providing a vital science victory backup since Iroquois already possess Apollo Program and we face a delegate deficit in the 10-turn Global Hegemony vote.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: Enlightenment
        - Rationale: Enlightenment is mandatory. Completing Rationalism in ~8 turns grants a free technology that can immediately unlock Satellites without consuming standard research time. +1 Happiness from Universities stabilizes our empire during active Cold War resolution and Iroquois suzerainty. No alternative policy offers comparable immediate value within this critical 10-turn window before World Congress voting.
    - #2:

Their 16 delegates represent the decisive swing vote in the upcoming Global Hegemony session—combined with our 29 delegates, Poland's support would yield 45 votes, exceeding the 41 threshold for immediate Diplomatic Victory. Strengthening this bond maximizes probability they vote for Indonesia rather than the Iroquois.

Priorities: (1) Maintain maximum Diplomacy/Gold/Religion (100/90/100) to protect four city-state alliances providing 8 delegates against Brazilian Great Diplomat strikes; (2) Raise Espionage to 85 to rig Belgrade elections and counter Brazilian influence; (3) Increase Spaceship to 70 and Science to 75 to prepare science backup, as Iroquois already possess Apollo Program; (4) Preserve extreme Defense/Nuke values (95/90) against Iroquois 377k military, border fort construction, and heavy bomber positioning near our cities. Global Peace Accords active means we must minimize offensive operations.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text:  Let me analyze the current situation carefully.
            
            **Critical Updates from Events:**
            1. **Zanzibar Alliance Flip**: Brazil used a Great Diplomat to take Zanzibar from me on Turn 424, but I immediately used my own Great Diplomat (ID: 18829) to reclaim Zanzibar on Turn 425. Zanzibar is now my ally again with 667 influence. This is huge - I now have my 4 city-state allies back: Prague, Wittenberg, Winchester, and Zanzibar = 8 delegates.
            
            2. **Diplomatic Victory Status**: 
               - VotesNeeded: 41
               - World Congress voting in 10 turns
               - Current delegates: Indonesia 29, Iroquois 33, Brazil 26
               - The proposal is "Global Hegemony" - automatically proposed when World Ideology is active
               - We have World Ideology: Autocracy enacted (gives us +1 delegate, plus 1 for every other civ following Autocracy)
            
            3. **Wars**: Still at war with Aztecs and Shoshone. The Iroquois are my suzerain (capitulated vassal), which is extremely dangerous.
            
            4. **Key City-State Quests**:
               - Prague: Hinduism majority quest has 28 turns remaining
               - Wittenberg: Great Musician quest has 16 turns remaining
               - Winchester: Great Artist quest has 41 turns remaining
               - Belgrade: Hinduism majority quest has 12 turns remaining, Great Admiral quest has 50 turns
            
            5. **Religious Situation**: Hinduism is my founded religion. Many Iroquois cities have been converted to Buddhism (Cork, Cardiff, etc. converted this turn). My cities Jakarta, Batam, Medan, Tlatelolco are Hindu.
            
            6. **Military**: I have 21,958 military strength vs Iroquois 377,574. This is a massive gap. We are a vassal of Iroquois. The Iroquois have atomic bombs and overwhelming military.
            
            7. **Strategic Goal**: Survive and win Diplomatic Victory. With 29 delegates vs 33 for Iroquois and 41 needed, I cannot win this vote. However, if the vote fails, the top two civilizations permanently gain 5 additional delegates. Since Brazil has 26, Poland 16, Aztecs 10, Shoshone 6, I will likely be in top 2. Actually wait - if no one gets 41, the two civs with MOST support get +5 delegates permanently.
            
            Let me check: Iroquois 33, me 29, Brazil 26. If no one wins, Iroquois and I get +5 each. Then I'd have 34, Iroquois 38, Brazil 26. Next vote I'd need 41... still not enough.
            
            Wait, but the key is securing enough delegates to eventually win. With +5 each failed vote, we inch closer. But we need to prevent Iroquois from winning outright.
            
            Actually, looking more carefully at the delegate counts and the tight race:
            - The Iroquois have 33 delegates (80% of needed)
            - I have 29 (70%)
            - Brazil has 26 (63%)
            
            If I can get to 41 delegates, I win. Currently I get delegates from:
            - Base delegates
            - World Ideology Autocracy (1 + 1 per Autocracy follower)
            - City-state allies: Prague (2), Wittenberg (2), Winchester (2), Zanzibar (2) = 8 delegates from city-states
            - Possible additional from being host or other sources
            
            Wait, looking at the last report, I have 29 delegates. With 8 from city-states, the remaining 21 come from other sources (population, base, World Religion if I'm founder, etc.). 
            
            The Iroquois have 33 - they likely get delegates from city-states too, plus their massive population/territory gives them more base delegates.
            
            **Critical Decision Points:**
            
            1. **Flavors/Grand Strategy**: Should maintain UnitedNations grand strategy. Need high Gold and Diplomacy to buy influence with city-states. Need high Defense since we're surrounded by Iroquois military and active wars with Aztecs/Shoshone.
            
            2. **Technology**: Currently researching Advanced Ballistics (3 turns left). Next was set to Electronics, but with only 10 turns until World Congress, we might need something else? Actually Electronics unlocks Police Station which helps with City Security and resistance. But given we're a vassal and there's World Congress in 10 turns... Satellites might be better for science backup. Actually, no - keep Electronics for security against potential rebellions or Iroquois aggression.
            
            3. **Policy**: Next was Enlightenment (Rationalism) - free technology. This is still excellent. Available in ~8 turns (from previous report). Actually current report says NextPolicyTurns: 8. But that was from earlier. Now the report says SciencePerTurn: 1811, CulturePerTurn: 2002. With 8 turns until policy, Enlightenment is great.
            
            4. **Relationships**: 
               - We reclaimed Zanzibar! Need to keep it.
               - Brazil is aggressively competing for city-states. They used a Great Diplomat on Zanzibar.
               - Iroquois are existential threat.
            
            5. **Persona**: Keep diplomatic persona. However, looking at current flavors, we have very high Defense (95), CityDefense (90), Nuke (90), etc. Given Iroquois military is overwhelming and we're their vassal, we need to maintain strong defensive posture while pursuing diplomatic victory.
            
            Wait, looking at events, the Iroquois are building forts right on my borders! Workers are constructing forts at (62,15), (54,29), and other tiles adjacent to my territory. They also have massive air force (Heavy Bombers, Zeros) in Brasilia, Yokohama, Surabaya, Palatium, and other cities near my territory. This is extremely threatening.
            
            Also, the Open Door resolution passed on Turn 425 - this was my proposal! It helps with city-state influence.
            
            **Zanzibar Trade Route Quest**: Zanzibar wants a land or sea trade route to their city (6 turns remaining). I should make sure I have a trade route going there. Actually, looking at my trade routes, I don't see one to Zanzibar. This could give 173 influence and 115 food in capital - not huge but helpful.
            
            **Critical Quest - Wittenberg Hinduism**: The previous rationale mentioned Wittenberg Hinduism quest had only 4 turns remaining. But looking at the current report for Wittenberg, I don't see the Hinduism quest listed! The only quest is "generate a Great Musician" with 16 turns. This means either:
            1. The Hinduism quest was completed
            2. The Hinduism quest expired
            3. Something changed
            
            Actually wait - looking at the current Wittenberg entry:
            ```
            - Quests:
              - They want you to generate a Great Musician. (Turns remaining: 16) Potential Quest Reward: * 150 Influence * 476 Culture
            ```
            
            The Hinduism quest is GONE. This is fantastic - it means Hinduism is likely already the majority religion in Wittenberg! Looking at Wittenberg data, it doesn't show MajorityReligion explicitly (unlike some others), but the absence of the quest suggests it's complete.
            
            Similarly, Prague has:
            ```
            - Quests:
              - They want Hinduism to become the majority religion in their city. (Turns remaining: 28) Potential Quest Reward: * 202 Influence * 259 Faith
            ```
            
            So Prague still needs Hinduism. But Zanzibar has MajorityReligion: Protestantism, so that's a concern but we still have alliance.
            
            **Key strategic decisions needed:**
            
            1. **Flavors**: Maintain UnitedNations grand strategy. Current flavors are heavily defensive. Given Iroquois fort-building on borders and massive military presence, this makes sense. But should I increase Gold and Diplomacy even more to secure city-states? With Brazil at 8228 gold and +708 GPT, they can outspend me on diplomatic units. I have 411 gold and -296 GPT, which is very dangerous.
            
            Actually my GPT is -296! That's terrible. I'm losing money fast. But my cities generate good gold (Jakarta 1008 GPT alone). Wait, the -296 is probably net after expenses. Still, with 411 gold, I don't have much buying power.
            
            Looking at deals:
            - Brazil gives me 80 GPT for 4 Coal (16 turns remaining)
            - Various deals give me GPT from Brazil, Poland
            - I'm paying GPT to Brazil for Aluminum and Brazilwood
            
            My economy is stretched. But I need diplomatic units to maintain alliances.
            
            2. **Research**: Keep Electronics. Police Stations provide +10 City Security and +3 Culture. With New World Order and Police State tenets, they get built faster. City security is critical when we're a vassal surrounded by Iroquois.
            
            3. **Policy**: Keep Enlightenment. Free tech is incredibly valuable, and +1 happiness from universities helps.
            
            4. **Relationships**:
               - Brazil: Currently -50 public, -60 private. Brazil is diplomatic rival. They stole Zanzibar (temporarily). But I just took it back. Brazil has massive economy. Should keep negative but not too aggressive - they're also a vassal of Iroquois.
               - Iroquois: -100/-100 is correct. They're existential threat.
               - Aztecs: -100/-100. At war with them, they took Denpasar.
               - Shoshone: -100/-100. At war.
               - Poland: +40/+20. Friendly, both Autocracy, useful ally.
               - City-states: All maxed positive.
            
            Should I adjust any relationships? Brazil relationship was set to -50/-60. Given Brazil just tried to steal Zanzibar and has 26 delegates, keeping negative pressure is correct.
            
            One thing: I notice I have a Declaration of Friendship with Aztecs and Brazil in the relationships section, despite being at war with Aztecs and having negative opinions of Brazil. This is likely because DoFs don't automatically break when war is declared in some cases, or the UI is showing historical data. My public opinion of Aztecs is -100 though.
            
            Wait, looking at the diplomacy more carefully:
            - My relationship with Aztecs shows "Declaration of Friendship" in the relationships list but my opinion of them is -100/-100. The events show we made a deal (Allow Embassy, Resources) on Turn 425. This suggests we're actually NOT at war anymore? Or the events show peacetime deals?
            
            Looking at military zones, there are Aztec units near Tlatelolco and Tenochtitlan, but zone 1062 (Tenochtitlan) shows "Dominance: Friendly" and my FriendlyStrength is 12308 vs their NeutralStrength 68. That suggests I'm occupying or dominating that area. Tlatelolco is my city (captured from Aztecs, ID 17138, IsOccupied: true).
            
            Actually, looking at the events, I don't see any combat events. But the strategy text explicitly says "active wars with Aztecs/Shoshone" and "losing cities during voting could cost us." However, no combat is reported in Events.
            
            Actually, the events show Aztec units moving near their own cities, and I have units there too. But no fighting occurred this turn.
            
            The war with Shoshone is confirmed by their denouncement and my -100 opinion.
            
            Wait, in the events I see:
            - Turn 425, Event 70: Deal with Aztecs - we trade Allow Embassy and Resources. This suggests we ARE at peace! You can't trade with someone you're at war with.
            - But the strategy text explicitly says "active wars with Aztecs/Shoshone"
            
            Hmm, let me re-read the events... Event 70 says:
            ```
            - Type: DealMade
            - StartTurn: 425
            - TradedItems:
            ...
            - ToOpponent: 5: The Aztecs
            - FromOpponent: 2: Indonesia
            ```
            
            We traded with Aztecs! That means we are at peace with them. But the previous strategy rationale said we were at war. This is a significant development - the war with Aztecs may have ended, or we were never at war and the previous rationale was mistaken. But Tlatelolco is IsOccupied, meaning we captured it from them. So we were at war, made peace, and now have an embassy and trade resources.
            
            Similarly, I don't see any war events with Shoshone in the current events, but they denounced us and have -100 relationship.
            
            Wait, the deal with Aztecs includes Allow Embassy from both sides, and resources (Ivory and Nutmeg?). This is a peace deal or a trade deal. StartTurn 425, Duration 50 turns for resources. This looks like a normal trade deal, which requires peace.
            
            So if we're at peace with Aztecs, that's good news - one less front to worry about. But the strategy text explicitly said "active wars with Aztecs/Shoshone." Perhaps the peace happened very recently.
            
            Actually, looking at the OutgoingTradeRoutes, I have trade routes to "Tlatelolco (Indonesia)" which is my city. And to The Iroquois cities. No trade routes to Aztec cities.
            
            But Event 70 says we made a deal with Aztecs. So we are at peace.
            
            Wait, I need to check if there's a peace treaty or just a trade deal. The deal includes AllowEmbassy and Resources with 50 duration. This is definitely a peaceful trade deal.
            
            So my war situation might just be with Shoshone now, not Aztecs. That's a significant improvement.
            
            However, Iroquois are still the massive threat. They have our capital (Jakarta was taken? No wait, Jakarta is listed as my city with ID 1059. But the Iroquois control capitals including Jakarta in the Domination Victory section! Wait:
            
            Domination Victory section says:
            - The Iroquois control: Onondaga, Jakarta, Warsaw, Kyoto, Tenochtitlan, Rio de Janeiro, Edinburgh
            
            JAKARTA is listed as controlled by Iroquois! But my cities section shows Jakarta as my city! How can this be?
            
            Wait, my original capital might have been Jakarta and it was captured by Iroquois, then I recaptured it or it's showing current ownership. Let me check: Jakarta is shown as Owner: Indonesia in the Cities section. But Domination Victory says Iroquois control Jakarta. This might mean Iroquois have my ORIGINAL capital (Jakarta) as part of their domination progress, but I currently own it? No, if they control it, they own it. 
            
            Actually, looking very carefully: The Iroquois list includes "Jakarta" as a capital they control. But in MY cities list, Jakarta is my city with ID 1059. This is contradictory unless... wait, maybe the cities list shows only cities I currently own, and Jakarta is indeed mine. Then why does Domination say Iroquois control it?
            
            Possibility 1: The Domination Victory data is stale or showing historical control.
            Possibility 2: I recaptured Jakarta but the Iroquois still get credit? No.
            Possibility 3: There's another Jakarta? No.
            
            Actually, looking at the Iroquois cities list, I don't see Jakarta there. The Iroquois have cities like Onondaga, Warsaw, Kyoto, etc. but NOT Jakarta in their city list. So the DominationVictory section's "CapitalsControlled" might be referring to the original capital cities' current owners. If Iroquois "control" Jakarta in domination terms, that means they own it. But I also have Jakarta in my cities list.
            
            Wait, looking again at Iroquois cities: They have "Surabaya" (ID 12603) with IsOccupied: true! Surabaya was my city! And "Palatium" (ID 13442) IsOccupied. "Edinburgh", "Warsaw", "Tokyo", etc. But Jakarta is NOT in the Iroquois city list.
            
            This is confusing. Perhaps the DominationVictory data has a bug or is using stale information. But the Iroquois do have Surabaya (my former city), so they've taken some of my cities.
            
            Regardless, the Iroquois control 87% of original capitals. They dominate militarily. We are their vassal.
            
            **Decision Framework:**
            
            Given:
            - 10 turns until World Congress vote on Global Hegemony
            - Need 41 votes to win
            - Have 29 votes, Iroquois have 33
            - Cannot win this vote unless I gain 12 more delegates in 10 turns (nearly impossible)
            - If vote fails, top 2 get +5 permanent delegates
            - Need to survive as #2 or #1 to get the +5 bonus
            - Must avoid being eliminated or having Iroquois win outright
            - Current delegates break ties are critical
            
            **What can I do in 10 turns?**
            - Complete Prague's Hinduism quest? No, 28 turns remaining.
            - Get more city-state allies? Belgrade is at 434 influence (friends, not ally, Iroquois ally at 660). Hard to overcome in 10 turns without Great Diplomat (I just used mine on Zanzibar).
            - Ally with Raqmu, Cape Town, Sokoto? Brazil controls these with high influence.
            - Generate Great Musician for Wittenberg quest? 16 turns remaining.
            - Generate Great Artist for Winchester? 41 turns.
            
            **Best outcome for this vote**: Ensure Iroquois don't get 41 votes, and ensure I am in top 2. Since I have 29 and Brazil has 26, and vote splitting occurs, I'm likely to be top 2 with Iroquois. But I need to make sure other civs don't surpass me.
            
            Brazil has 26 delegates and strong economy. They could potentially bribe some city-states or get support. But I'm 3 ahead.
            
            Wait, the resolution mechanics for Global Hegemony: It says "Designate a civilization that could achieve Global Hegemony. If a civilization receives support from enough delegates... that leader will attain Diplomatic Victory... If no civilization receives enough support, the two civilizations who received the most support permanently gain 5 additional delegates."
            
            So this isn't just about having delegates - it's about VOTING. Each delegate can vote for someone or abstain. Iroquois might get votes from their vassals (Aztecs, Brazil are also their vassals!). Wait, Brazil and Aztecs are vassals of Iroquois (capitulated). 
            
            If Iroquois can command their vassals' votes, they control Brazil (26) + Aztecs (10) + themselves (33) = 69 delegates! But wait, vassals don't automatically vote for their master. In Civ5, vassals can vote independently in World Congress. However, they might be influenced.
            
            Actually, looking at Brazil's info: they have Autocracy too (like me and Iroquois). They might vote for themselves or Iroquois.
            
            If Iroquois get support from Aztecs and Poland, they might reach 33+16+10 = 59, well over 41.
            
            But the AI doesn't always coordinate perfectly. Still, this is extremely dangerous.
            
            Wait, if Brazil, Aztecs, and I are all vassals of Iroquois... that's 26+10+29 = 65 delegates under Iroquois suzerainty. If the Iroquois can consolidate these, they win easily. But vassals have some autonomy.
            
            Actually, looking at the relationships: Poland has Defensive Pact and Declaration of Friendship with Iroquois, but Poland is NOT a vassal. Brazil, Aztecs, and Indonesia ARE vassals.
            
            If Iroquois vote for themselves and tell their vassals to vote for them, they get 33+26+10+29 = 98 votes, way more than 41. They win.
            
            But the AI might not be that coordinated. And I might vote for myself. The question is: can I get others to vote for me?
